I am Marc, a radical thinker, into simple living

Flove.org is my life long project. A wholistic theory integralized
with practical miniapps.

I simplify fundamentals for the more fundamental fields (...)

I am humble while i have to be very adventorous, so I expect me to
be not just "a bit" wrong or right, but "very" wrong & right…

Enjoy and Flaw on.

    RESUME OF POPULAR FLAWS WITHIN THE ACHADEMIA
1-FREEDOM is used to bully DESTINY as FATALISM, with a strawman - false dilemma fallacy.
rather than for the “Worst possible Universe” option at “Many Worlds, Beyond the Standard Model”

2-DETERMINISM is rather used for REDUCTIONISM “finiting within an infinite ENTROPY” (while
 it’s the other way round) despite the MEASSUREMENT PROBLEM and else UNDEFINABILITIES
evidenced at every High and Low Scientific fields. Forces the adding “HIPOTHESIS” to
TELEOLOGIES and “SUPER”DETERMINISM, “THEOLOGICAL” DETERMINISM (GOD) andor
(HARD) INCOMPATIBILISM which is rather used for justifying FATALIST Monotheisms.

3-FINE TUNNING & ANTHROPIC PRINCIPLE VARIANTS (a misnomer for TELEOLOGIES),
specially the STRONG/PARTICIPATORY Variant, are ignored and buzzied at the same time.

4-THE UNCERTAINITY PRINCIPLE is applied to TELEOLOGY while it is rather meant to be
applied tor OBSERVATION.
4.1-EVOLUTIONARY BIOLOGY MISSING LINKS & UNPREDICTABILITY is used for justifying a
RANDOM TELEOLOGY. Religion Historiography is now bullied andor for used for Monotheisms.

5-FREEDOM and WILL are Rupturists-Specists (Flaw: Other matters are determined but not us...)
and both are not rigorously evidenced andso buzzily defined with “Cognition – Consciousnes - etc”.

 WILL           Mandamientos y Pecados
   En la DEONTEOLOGIA CRISTIANA
             Hay un orden linear para 10 Mandamientos
                  y no lo hay para los 7 Pecados.

  Hay muchas versiones y debates sobre el orden de cada cual, pero
   pocas agrupaciones y relaciones simples y directas entre ellos.


       Pues disfruta mas la siguiente propuesta de
Bipolaridades entre Mandamientos y Pecados (y mas retos)
                       CATÓLICOS (por mas sintéticos)
                       RESUMIDOS (por reducir espacio y popularizarlos)

                       EN ESPAÑOL (por historia cercana)

     Case: PROSODY

               We value the analogy of our relation with the relation of the object with its
               Prosody (more or less correlated or contradictory). We also value PROSODY
               more than the OBJECT




  PROPOSITION

OBJECT
JOIN           PROSODY
ME!




       I       YOU
           1
                     LINGUISTIC CHANNELS display a more simplex
                     management of Space (Proxemics) & time (Chronemics).
                     We evolve novel channels by involving (preserving)
                     ancestor channels in the new ones, this way reaching
                     new micros inversely too.

                     I.e. At Textual Channel (aka see: “JOIN ME!”) there’s
                     always an inner PROSODY (aka “!”) that simplexifies all
                     ancestor Channels within the newer Textual one is.
  PROPOSITION
                     PROSODY refers more to the inners of the propositor
OBJECT               than the Object, so is fundamental for andor influences
JOIN
ME!
           PROSODY   more its Semantics. So textual objects serve more for
                     expressing PROSODY, more than the other way round.

                                   ABOUT ME

I am Marc, a radical thinker, into simple living

Flove.org is my life long project. A wholistic theory integralized
with practical miniapps.

I simplify fundamentals for the more fundamental fields (...)

I am humble while i have to be very adventorous, so I expect me to
be not just "a bit" wrong or right, but "very" wrong & right…

Enjoy and Flaw on.

    RESUME OF POPULAR FLAWS WITHIN THE ACHADEMIA
1-FREEDOM is used to bully DESTINY as FATALISM, with a strawman - false dilemma fallacy.
rather than for the “Worst possible Universe” option at “Many Worlds, Beyond the Standard Model”

2-DETERMINISM is rather used for REDUCTIONISM “finiting within an infinite ENTROPY” (while
 it’s the other way round) despite the MEASSUREMENT PROBLEM and else UNDEFINABILITIES
evidenced at every High and Low Scientific fields. Forces the adding “HIPOTHESIS” to
TELEOLOGIES and “SUPER”DETERMINISM, “THEOLOGICAL” DETERMINISM (GOD) andor
(HARD) INCOMPATIBILISM which is rather used for justifying FATALIST Monotheisms.

3-FINE TUNNING & ANTHROPIC PRINCIPLE VARIANTS (a misnomer for TELEOLOGIES),
specially the STRONG/PARTICIPATORY Variant, are ignored and buzzied at the same time.

4-THE UNCERTAINITY PRINCIPLE is applied to TELEOLOGY while it is rather meant to be
applied tor OBSERVATION.
4.1-EVOLUTIONARY BIOLOGY MISSING LINKS & UNPREDICTABILITY is used for justifying a
RANDOM TELEOLOGY. Religion Historiography is now bullied andor for used for Monotheisms.

5-FREEDOM and WILL are Rupturists-Specists (Flaw: Other matters are determined but not us...)
and both are not rigorously evidenced andso buzzily defined with “Cognition – Consciousnes - etc”.

 WILL           Mandamientos y Pecados
   En la DEONTEOLOGIA CRISTIANA
             Hay un orden linear para 10 Mandamientos
                  y no lo hay para los 7 Pecados.

  Hay muchas versiones y debates sobre el orden de cada cual, pero
   pocas agrupaciones y relaciones simples y directas entre ellos.


       Pues disfruta mas la siguiente propuesta de
Bipolaridades entre Mandamientos y Pecados (y mas retos)
                       CATÓLICOS (por mas sintéticos)
                       RESUMIDOS (por reducir espacio y popularizarlos)

                       EN ESPAÑOL (por historia cercana)

                     LINGUISTIC CHANNELS display a more simplex
                     management of Space (Proxemics) & time (Chronemics).
                     We evolve novel channels by involving (preserving)
                     ancestor channels in the new ones, this way reaching
                     new micros inversely too.

                     I.e. At Textual Channel (aka see: “JOIN ME!”) there’s
                     always an inner PROSODY (aka “!”) that simplexifies all
                     ancestor Channels within the newer Textual one is.

                     expressing PROSODY, more than the other way round.
